# B0248 — Preparing collective occasions

B0248 follows three complete Event families through the work that makes a collective occasion possible: preparation for Tokyo-7th sports, Le☆S☆Ca's making-of recording, and Nanasta's Christmas preparations. Their common subject is not a uniform team spirit but the negotiation of contribution, representation and knowledge. Chacha's support work can motivate Ei while remaining undervalued by a manager's training-based image of effort; Sisara's shared-win intention coexists with Makoto's attention pressure and local gratitude. LeSca's peers supply real encouragement and practical care while contesting camera framing and bodily jokes. Christmas gives Haru and Kajika creative energy around their limited collaboration and a theme they chose, yet leaves other members' act/costume preferences unresolved. The final narration closes preparation without staging the party. These differences prevent a general cure, universal consent or a cross-family calendar from replacing the actual scenes.

Player says the running image is unfamiliar to him and asks if she faced something frightening after she says she used to flee various things as a child. Ei tentatively accepts the scary description while saying she has become accustomed. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0179.json__71bcf94604db0689?doc=primary&log=8#/Pages/8`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0179.json__71bcf94604db0689?doc=primary&log=15#/Pages/15`.

These are her retrospective accounts, not a pictured childhood sequence or independently supplied ghost speech. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0179.json__71bcf94604db0689?doc=primary&log=16#/Pages/16`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0179.json__71bcf94604db0689?doc=primary&log=20#/Pages/20`.

White fade ends the exchange without a run, relay, race time or settled common explanation. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0179.json__71bcf94604db0689?doc=primary&log=21#/Pages/21`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0179.json__71bcf94604db0689?doc=primary&log=25#/Pages/25`.

No possession or battle is shown. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0180.json__ed1055ee1e080cc4?doc=primary&log=0#/Pages/0`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0180.json__ed1055ee1e080cc4?doc=primary&log=11#/Pages/11`.

#### Interpretation and alternatives

**CL-B0248-015 — The scene moves from attributed sporting strength into a borrowed heroic script**

The scene moves from attributed sporting strength into a borrowed heroic script. Jeda uses Matsuri's reported lesson as an imaginative frame for participation, and Ei predicts victory from her appraisal. These are active choices and supportive enthusiasm, not a verified unbeaten result or evidence that historical language confers skill. Matsuri is a named source of advice through Jeda's account, with no own teaching turn or hidden objective supplied.

**CL-B0248-016 — The previous spirit conversation makes Player's literal reading of 心が宿る available, but Ei immediately corrects the extension to summoning**

The previous spirit conversation makes Player's literal reading of 心が宿る available, but Ei immediately corrects the extension to summoning. Perception of spirits and the power to call a historical figure are not interchangeable capabilities. Jeda's next line and Player's uptake settle this particular phrase as an internalized teaching in the conversation; they do not erase the corpus's admitted supernatural events or establish total knowledge of all spiritual mechanisms.

**CL-B0248-017 — Chacha creates the Kyoko rivalry through a name/allusion connection**

Chacha creates the Kyoko rivalry through a name/allusion connection. The scene differentiates interpretive access: she cues, Ei answers, Player recognizes the point, Jeda asks for explanation and then retrieves the full name. Player's failure is to make Chacha's connection at that moment, not proof that he has forgotten Kyoko altogether. The help-boat offer blends teaching and a pointed reminder of managerial familiarity.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0180.json__ed1055ee1e080cc4?doc=primary&log=0#/Pages/0`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0180.json__ed1055ee1e080cc4?doc=primary&log=11#/Pages/11`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0180.json__ed1055ee1e080cc4?doc=primary&log=12#/Pages/12`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0180.json__ed1055ee1e080cc4?doc=primary&log=22#/Pages/22`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0180.json__ed1055ee1e080cc4?doc=primary&log=23#/Pages/23`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0180.json__ed1055ee1e080cc4?doc=primary&log=28#/Pages/28`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0180.json__ed1055ee1e080cc4?doc=primary&log=29#/Pages/29`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0180.json__ed1055ee1e080cc4?doc=primary&log=34#/Pages/34`.

**CL-B0248-018 — The salt story offers an ethical model of competition**

The salt story offers an ethical model of competition. In Chacha's reading, helping a weakened opponent allows a fair contest; Jeda explicitly wants that kind of fight. The scene can value respect between rivals without claiming that any participant has yet enacted fairness, that every historical detail is independently established or that Kyoko has agreed to the imposed frame.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0180.json__ed1055ee1e080cc4?doc=primary&log=0#/Pages/0`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0180.json__ed1055ee1e080cc4?doc=primary&log=11#/Pages/11`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0180.json__ed1055ee1e080cc4?doc=primary&log=12#/Pages/12`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0180.json__ed1055ee1e080cc4?doc=primary&log=22#/Pages/22`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0180.json__ed1055ee1e080cc4?doc=primary&log=23#/Pages/23`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0180.json__ed1055ee1e080cc4?doc=primary&log=28#/Pages/28`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0180.json__ed1055ee1e080cc4?doc=primary&log=29#/Pages/29`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0180.json__ed1055ee1e080cc4?doc=primary&log=34#/Pages/34`.

**CL-B0248-019 — Ei's concern and Player's objection preserve the cost of turning another person's name into entertainment**

Ei's concern and Player's objection preserve the cost of turning another person's name into entertainment. Kyoko is not here to accept the rival role or supply her own appraisal. Chacha can enjoy the expected encounter while also distributing its burden to others; her useful explanatory care is not blanket proof of consent-respecting conduct. The chapter's own objections keep that issue visible rather than requiring an external moral verdict.

**CL-B0248-020 — The closing salt request converts an analogy back into a literal prop even as Ei identifies the missing need**

The closing salt request converts an analogy back into a literal prop even as Ei identifies the missing need. Jeda's enthusiasm is creative and socially engaged, but the declared errand is not its execution. This continues the family’s concern with how stories inform preparation: Makoto's anime mechanisms, Ei's childhood explanation and Jeda's samurai framework have different evidentiary statuses and practical limits.

Allusion crosses Japanese name knowledge, remembered popular history, borrowed cultural forms and Jeda's textual English insertions ホワイ/クール/フェアファイト. #### Interpretation and alternatives

**CL-B0248-022 — The practical help request gives the event a material scale**

The practical help request gives the event a material scale. Sisara knows what sport she will play and the signs she needs, but lacks a footwear decision and struggles with coordination timing. Her request and later explicit delegation make shared competence possible without pretending that Player is an expert. His own second thoughts preserve the difficulty rather than letting managerial reassurance become a certified technical solution.

**CL-B0248-023 — Sisara's team wording matters, and Player explicitly values it**

Sisara's team wording matters, and Player explicitly values it. Yet the comparison with a wholly self-reliant Sisara is his parenthesized appraisal within this episode. Published earlier help requests, shared rehearsal, chosen Nanasta labor and professional-pressure histories are already admitted, with unresolved global chronology. This scene adds another situated choice to cooperate; it cannot be announced as her first capacity for trust or the final cure of insecurity and coercion costs.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0181.json__4a07c682a22bdded?doc=primary&log=0#/Pages/0`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0181.json__4a07c682a22bdded?doc=primary&log=9#/Pages/9`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0181.json__4a07c682a22bdded?doc=primary&log=10#/Pages/10`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0181.json__4a07c682a22bdded?doc=primary&log=24#/Pages/24`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0181.json__4a07c682a22bdded?doc=primary&log=25#/Pages/25`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0181.json__4a07c682a22bdded?doc=primary&log=30#/Pages/30`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0181.json__4a07c682a22bdded?doc=primary&log=31#/Pages/31`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0181.json__4a07c682a22bdded?doc=primary&log=41#/Pages/41`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0181.json__4a07c682a22bdded?doc=primary&log=42#/Pages/42`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0181.json__4a07c682a22bdded?doc=primary&log=60#/Pages/60`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0181.json__4a07c682a22bdded?doc=primary&log=61#/Pages/61`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0181.json__4a07c682a22bdded?doc=primary&log=70#/Pages/70`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0181.json__4a07c682a22bdded?doc=primary&log=71#/Pages/71`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0181.json__4a07c682a22bdded?doc=primary&log=77#/Pages/77`.

**CL-B0248-024 — Delegating a shoe choice is also a form of self-authorship**

Delegating a shoe choice is also a form of self-authorship. Sisara identifies her impasse and deliberately asks another person to decide, then later settles on an earlier suggestion and allocates a turn to Makoto. She does not simply lose agency to the manager. Her quoted general rule about overthinking is an actor's strategy, not a universal psychological law, and the exact chosen shoe is not established by an inspected product.

#### Interpretation and alternatives

**CL-B0248-032 — The ending completes a preparation-and-recognition story, not the sports competition named in its title**

The ending completes a preparation-and-recognition story, not the sports competition named in its title. Jeda supplies a later report that the planned run occurred, the shoppers return and the group restates its aims. No relay, cavalry battle, softball match, volleyball result or final ranking appears. The collective cheer has emotional and social force without functioning as evidence of victory.

- Ch1:P31-38 uncertainty about softball leads to media study as Makoto's chosen preparation. Ch4:P14/39/57/68 andCh5:P13 explicitly continue that study/practice concern; the progression does not prove useful sport technique, literal aura/fire or total correction.

Honoka's disclosure changes the others' account of what they saw. Kyoko had read the movement as an arrangement, and Rena now links originality to dizziness. Honoka's protest and turn to Rena are active conversational choices, not merely evidence that she is an incompetent or perpetually passive novice.

**CL-B0248-052 — The closing keeps representation and company entangled**

The closing keeps representation and company entangled. Rena takes the camera away, Kyoko pursues better visibility, and Honoka is left reflecting before hurrying after them. Those beats give three different relationships to the same project: initiating/documenting, managing appearance and learning exposure. No actor is reduced to a fixed role, and the local absence of a manager-own turn prevents attributing a full production policy or omniscient coaching plan.

Rena makes another body/posture joke; Kyoko objects, Honoka notes the repeated exchange and Rena contrasts making-of with a Kyoko image video. Honoka admires this and asks for encouragement at a live; Kyoko agrees and Rena joins the future effort. The reported change and its felt cause are not an independently audited take, diagnosis or total cure. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0184.json__3f677bd9eaff33bd?doc=primary&log=21#/Pages/21`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0184.json__3f677bd9eaff33bd?doc=primary&log=26#/Pages/26`.

#### Interpretation and alternatives

**CL-B0248-054 — Changing the camera holder changes who directs the conversation**

Changing the camera holder changes who directs the conversation. Honoka's explicit ウチ→私 repair is a small act of public self-presentation, followed by a chosen question about shared nerves. The source presents both ordinary linguistic habit and deliberate formality; it does not require two identities, a hierarchy of authentic voices or an analyst-heard accent.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0184.json__3f677bd9eaff33bd?doc=primary&log=0#/Pages/0`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0184.json__3f677bd9eaff33bd?doc=primary&log=10#/Pages/10`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0184.json__3f677bd9eaff33bd?doc=primary&log=11#/Pages/11`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0184.json__3f677bd9eaff33bd?doc=primary&log=20#/Pages/20`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0184.json__3f677bd9eaff33bd?doc=primary&log=21#/Pages/21`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0184.json__3f677bd9eaff33bd?doc=primary&log=26#/Pages/26`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0184.json__3f677bd9eaff33bd?doc=primary&log=27#/Pages/27`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0184.json__3f677bd9eaff33bd?doc=primary&log=36#/Pages/36`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0184.json__3f677bd9eaff33bd?doc=primary&log=37#/Pages/37`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0184.json__3f677bd9eaff33bd?doc=primary&log=40#/Pages/40`.

**CL-B0248-055 — The trio offers three positions toward a debut recording**

The trio offers three positions toward a debut recording. Rena admits nerves alongside stronger excitement; Kyoko articulates a duty to listeners and an everyday-work standard; Honoka describes crowding thoughts and looks for encouragement. These positions are not equivalent to objectively measured fear, resilience or artistic quality. Kyoko's principle can be substantive professional agency even when framed by her self-protective ease claim.

**CL-B0248-056 — Honoka's admiration and requested future encouragement give that professional speech a concrete interpersonal effect**

Honoka's admiration and requested future encouragement give that professional speech a concrete interpersonal effect. Kyoko's assent is a positive peer commitment. Her immediately following criticism of slow recording prevents an uncomplicated comfort-only model: the same peer can offer courage and maintain a demanding pace expectation. No blanket supportive/unsupportive label exhausts that exchange.

**CL-B0248-057 — Rena counters criticism with a reported improvement, and Honoka explains the change through relief at the manager's arrival**

Rena counters criticism with a reported improvement, and Honoka explains the change through relief at the manager's arrival. Her own felt experience is stronger evidence than an analyst inventing a rescue, and narrower than proof that his mere presence caused better singing or permanently solved overload. Other already admitted sources preserve Honoka's own craft, peer help and recovered light-facing purpose; this local acknowledgment cannot transfer all authorship of her agency to him.

**CL-B0248-058 — The manager's presence has unequal meanings**

The manager's presence has unequal meanings. Honoka values an arrival that the other two describe as too late for their recordings. A single supportive event for one performer can coexist with missed attention for others. Kyoko's 'always' remains her generalization, not a complete timekeeping audit; the current absence of the manager's own reply leaves cause, constraints and response unprovided.

**CL-B0248-059 — Rena and Kyoko turn complaint into an intended teasing lesson, but its justification does not make every proposed joke consensual or every allegation true**

Rena and Kyoko turn complaint into an intended teasing lesson, but its justification does not make every proposed joke consensual or every allegation true. Honoka actively supplies the work-role explanation for being around girls and cautions about the camera's future audience. Her intervention is neither passive agreement nor a claim that all criticism of management must be suppressed.

**CL-B0248-060 — Anticipated viewing makes the camera an accountability surface as well as a publicity tool**

Anticipated viewing makes the camera an accountability surface as well as a publicity tool. Honoka worries about the future recipient; Kyoko elects to let her asserted truth stand, and Rena enjoys the possibility. That choice can be candid criticism and comic risk without inventing an actual scandal, a retaliatory act or completed reception. The recording remains represented through dialogue rather than a delivered film.

This chapter develops the making-of project's contrast between polished image and the labor/relationships that make it possible. #### Factual reconstruction

- **EV-B0248-053 / P1-16:** Player reports preparations underway but himself without a task, and proposes visiting/supporting the others and buying missing supplies within a small budget. Haru and Kajika ask to accompany him. Their Christmas-limited two-person unit is described as a possible new-unit test, with a self-chosen theme and full planning task reported from Coney. Haru feels underwhelmed because singing/dancing seems familiar; Kajika wants encouragement from others' effort. Player accepts their company and chooses Sawara's practice to visit. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0188.json__d9e28b00dfe0f481?doc=primary&log=1#/Pages/1`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0188.json__d9e28b00dfe0f481?doc=primary&log=16#/Pages/16`.

Kajika sees dishes and asks about cooking; Sawara and Kajika make a culinary-ability exchange, without supplying an actual dish. Sawara identifies her act as tablecloth pulling. Player calls it difficult and warns conditionally about breakage. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0188.json__d9e28b00dfe0f481?doc=primary&log=17#/Pages/17`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0188.json__d9e28b00dfe0f481?doc=primary&log=31#/Pages/31`.
- **EV-B0248-055 / P32-43:** Sawara attempts the pull. Haru says only the cloth moved, Sawara calls it a success, Player says plates/glasses did not move and Kajika praises her. Sawara discloses previous practice, joking that she might someday face a foreign stranger's street challenge. Haru accepts the explanation; Player doubts that it normally happens. Shizuka interprets the trick as international exchange and expresses interest. No actual foreign encounter or cultural rule is established. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0188.json__d9e28b00dfe0f481?doc=primary&log=32#/Pages/32`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0188.json__d9e28b00dfe0f481?doc=primary&log=43#/Pages/43`.
- **EV-B0248-056 / P44-59:** Sawara invites Shizuka to try and says the cloth/dishes belong to her household. Shizuka asks for advice, gets Sawara's onomatopoeic formulation despite Kajika's warning it may be unclear, repeats it and attempts the trick. Haru says everything broke, Shizuka admits failure, Sawara reassures her and reports earlier breakages during home practice. Kajika connects this with a remembered decline in household dishes. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0188.json__d9e28b00dfe0f481?doc=primary&log=44#/Pages/44`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0188.json__d9e28b00dfe0f481?doc=primary&log=59#/Pages/59`.

Haru fades66/clears67 and is absent through79. Sumire proposes plastic cups for practice, then identifies crystal/luxury tableware. Sawara says she suspected the expense by feel; Shizuka says these are ordinary household items. The prop value is unevenly known; no exact valuation or permission to destroy it is supplied. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0188.json__d9e28b00dfe0f481?doc=primary&log=60#/Pages/60`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0188.json__d9e28b00dfe0f481?doc=primary&log=76#/Pages/76`.

The visit gives Player a bounded support role rather than verifying his global idleness: his initial report concerns this preparation arrangement, and he elects company/supply support under a stated budget limit. #### Directional relationships in this interval

- **Haru → Kajika:** Shares a Christmas-limited two-person unit with Kajika; they choose its theme and are tasked by Coney to plan it. Choice of the pairing itself is unestablished, as are permanence and performance. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0188.json__d9e28b00dfe0f481?doc=primary&log=7#/Pages/7`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0188.json__d9e28b00dfe0f481?doc=primary&log=8#/Pages/8`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0188.json__d9e28b00dfe0f481?doc=primary&log=10#/Pages/10`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0188.json__d9e28b00dfe0f481?doc=primary&log=12#/Pages/12`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0188.json__d9e28b00dfe0f481?doc=primary&log=14#/Pages/14`.
- **Kajika → Haru:** Affirms the collaboration and desire for others' enthusiasm; temporary scope and distinct later roles remain. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0188.json__d9e28b00dfe0f481?doc=primary&log=7#/Pages/7`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0188.json__d9e28b00dfe0f481?doc=primary&log=8#/Pages/8`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0188.json__d9e28b00dfe0f481?doc=primary&log=11#/Pages/11`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0188.json__d9e28b00dfe0f481?doc=primary&log=12#/Pages/12`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0188.json__d9e28b00dfe0f481?doc=primary&log=14#/Pages/14`.
- **Sawara → Shizuka:** Invites an attempt with vague experiential instruction and normalizes earlier failures after breakage; no demonstrated complete technical teaching. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0188.json__d9e28b00dfe0f481?doc=primary&log=44#/Pages/44`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0188.json__d9e28b00dfe0f481?doc=primary&log=45#/Pages/45`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0188.json__d9e28b00dfe0f481?doc=primary&log=51#/Pages/51`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0188.json__d9e28b00dfe0f481?doc=primary&log=58#/Pages/58`.
- **Shizuka → Sawara and the group:** Offers reported household resources and attempts the skill, then recognizes failure; resources do not guarantee ability. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0188.json__d9e28b00dfe0f481?doc=primary&log=45#/Pages/45`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0188.json__d9e28b00dfe0f481?doc=primary&log=46#/Pages/46`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0188.json__d9e28b00dfe0f481?doc=primary&log=48#/Pages/48`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0188.json__d9e28b00dfe0f481?doc=primary&log=50#/Pages/50`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0188.json__d9e28b00dfe0f481?doc=primary&log=53#/Pages/53`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0188.json__d9e28b00dfe0f481?doc=primary&log=56#/Pages/56`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0188.json__d9e28b00dfe0f481?doc=primary&log=57#/Pages/57`.
- **Sumire → Haru and the group:** Turns noise and glass into practical cleanup needs and a plastic-cup suggestion; execution of all cleanup remains unshown. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0188.json__d9e28b00dfe0f481?doc=primary&log=60#/Pages/60`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0188.json__d9e28b00dfe0f481?doc=primary&log=62#/Pages/62`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0188.json__d9e28b00dfe0f481?doc=primary&log=63#/Pages/63`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0188.json__d9e28b00dfe0f481?doc=primary&log=64#/Pages/64`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0188.json__d9e28b00dfe0f481?doc=primary&log=65#/Pages/65`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0188.json__d9e28b00dfe0f481?doc=primary&log=67#/Pages/67`.
- **Haru → The group:** Volunteers for supplies, leaves via explicit absence sequence and returns saying she brought tools; no inference that absence erased all hearing or that cleanup is finished. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0188.json__d9e28b00dfe0f481?doc=primary&log=63#/Pages/63`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0188.json__d9e28b00dfe0f481?doc=primary&log=65#/Pages/65`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0188.json__d9e28b00dfe0f481?doc=primary&log=66#/Pages/66`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0188.json__d9e28b00dfe0f481?doc=primary&log=67#/Pages/67`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0188.json__d9e28b00dfe0f481?doc=primary&log=79#/Pages/79`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0188.json__d9e28b00dfe0f481?doc=primary&log=80#/Pages/80`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0188.json__d9e28b00dfe0f481?doc=primary&log=81#/Pages/81`.

#### Source-local character changes and original relational qualifications

- **Player:** Elects visiting/supply support within a small-budget account, witnesses local success/breakage, questions the hypothetical and asks an uncertain valuation. No audited idleness, expert safety assurance, agreed repayment or clinical shock. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0188.json__d9e28b00dfe0f481?doc=primary&log=1#/Pages/1`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0188.json__d9e28b00dfe0f481?doc=primary&log=2#/Pages/2`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0188.json__d9e28b00dfe0f481?doc=primary&log=4#/Pages/4`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0188.json__d9e28b00dfe0f481?doc=primary&log=6#/Pages/6`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0188.json__d9e28b00dfe0f481?doc=primary&log=9#/Pages/9`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0188.json__d9e28b00dfe0f481?doc=primary&log=13#/Pages/13`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0188.json__d9e28b00dfe0f481?doc=primary&log=15#/Pages/15`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0188.json__d9e28b00dfe0f481?doc=primary&log=26#/Pages/26`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0188.json__d9e28b00dfe0f481?doc=primary&log=29#/Pages/29`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0188.json__d9e28b00dfe0f481?doc=primary&log=31#/Pages/31`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0188.json__d9e28b00dfe0f481?doc=primary&log=35#/Pages/35`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0188.json__d9e28b00dfe0f481?doc=primary&log=42#/Pages/42`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0188.json__d9e28b00dfe0f481?doc=primary&log=47#/Pages/47`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0188.json__d9e28b00dfe0f481?doc=primary&log=55#/Pages/55`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0188.json__d9e28b00dfe0f481?doc=primary&log=69#/Pages/69`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0188.json__d9e28b00dfe0f481?doc=primary&log=74#/Pages/74`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0188.json__d9e28b00dfe0f481?doc=primary&log=77#/Pages/77`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0188.json__d9e28b00dfe0f481?doc=primary&log=79#/Pages/79`.

- **Sawara:** Shows textually witnessed tablecloth skill, discloses earlier practice/breakage, offers a hypothetical motive, invites Shizuka and gives opaque advice/reassurance. #### Interpretation and alternatives

**CL-B0248-099 — The initial concealed object of disagreement invites a risk explanation, but Shinju corrects that diagnosis herself**

The initial concealed object of disagreement invites a risk explanation, but Shinju corrects that diagnosis herself. She distinguishes trust in Shizuka's tested skill from objection to the proposed costume. Taking her answer seriously preserves a specific preference rather than recoding all resistance as fear, ignorance, shyness or a condition already cured by reassurance.

**CL-B0248-100 — Risk judgments remain actor-local**

Risk judgments remain actor-local. Haru's concern, Shizuka's suction-tip account, Kajika's reassurance and Shinju's confidence after reported test shots are separate information routes. They do not constitute an analyst's inspected shot, safety certification or proof that any equipment is harmless. Sawara's imagined interference is a sibling forecast, not a new harmful act or universal incapacity.

**CL-B0248-101 — The costume dispute separates the planned act's assistant function from the visibility desired for its performer**

The costume dispute separates the planned act's assistant function from the visibility desired for its performer. Shinju uses a source-story role to challenge the fairy choice, while Shizuka values a more striking/cute presentation over literal role fidelity. Their disagreement is intelligible without adjudicating the external legend or inventing a historical costume standard.

**CL-B0248-102 — Shizuka's unusual firmness qualifies a uniformly accommodating image without cancelling her agency or previous care**

Shizuka's unusual firmness qualifies a uniformly accommodating image without cancelling her agency or previous care. She actively designs the act, offers a representation of her partner to an audience and recruits household resources. Her positive appraisal of Shinju does not by itself authorize that representation. A wish to show someone attractively can coexist with overriding their expressed preference.

**CL-B0248-103 — Kajika turns from an expected advocate into another eager audience member**

Kajika turns from an expected advocate into another eager audience member. Shinju names that failed appeal and continues to object. The difference matters because sisterly affection and knowledge of cuteness do not merge preferences or confer permission. Kajika's enthusiasm is real, yet it remains hers, just as Shizuka's judgment remains hers.

**CL-B0248-105 — Player is asked for help but no successful intervention or negotiated resolution is supplied**

Player is asked for help but no successful intervention or negotiated resolution is supplied. He questions the risk, receives a correction, responds to the legend account and acknowledges cost. Haru's final sympathy likewise does not secure the requested support: she adds her own wish to see the outfit. The ending broadens the audience coalition around a continuing objection rather than proving secret consent or completed coercion.

**CL-B0248-106 — The prior chapter's failed tablecloth attempt cannot become global Shizuka incompetence: here Shinju reports confidence in another practiced domain**

The prior chapter's failed tablecloth attempt cannot become global Shizuka incompetence: here Shinju reports confidence in another practiced domain. Conversely, skill and resource provision do not guarantee adequate consent. Domain-specific capability, social insistence, amusement and costs can coexist in the same character without a total reversal or maturity promotion.

This chapter supplies preparation and negotiated representation, not the show itself. Ordinary portraits and a costume-room background frame discussion, but no fairy/William Tell outfit, apple, active bow shot or result is inspected. Shinju's objection remains live at the before-next-chapter freeze, and any later outcome must be recorded as a transition rather than backdated.

#### Source-local character changes and original relational qualifications

- **Shinju:** Distinguishes confidence in archery from costume disagreement, gives a source-story account, seeks advocacy, raises cost and repeatedly rejects others deciding for her. The central comic mechanism is a confidence gap. Enthusiasm and remembered spectacle outrun technical understanding; Sui repeatedly treats the word illusion and trust in Momoka as guarantees, while Momoka explicitly lacks the trick and makes success probabilistic. - **Susu → Rona:** Adds a remembered chain element and offers to help; enthusiasm is her own and does not settle Rona's fear. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0191.json__f46c72674e9980a9?doc=primary&log=59#/Pages/59`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0191.json__f46c72674e9980a9?doc=primary&log=60#/Pages/60`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0191.json__f46c72674e9980a9?doc=primary&log=74#/Pages/74`.
- **Susu → Player:** Calls for a hug before his explicit separation request; do not infer mutual romantic assent, actual release or injury. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0191.json__f46c72674e9980a9?doc=primary&log=14#/Pages/14`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0191.json__f46c72674e9980a9?doc=primary&log=15#/Pages/15`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0191.json__f46c72674e9980a9?doc=primary&log=16#/Pages/16`.
- **Momoka → Haru:** Corrects apparent hypnosis ability to a sleep-technique joke, which Haru recognizes; no explicit stage direction makes this private or excludes other hearers. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0191.json__f46c72674e9980a9?doc=primary&log=77#/Pages/77`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0191.json__f46c72674e9980a9?doc=primary&log=78#/Pages/78`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0191.json__f46c72674e9980a9?doc=primary&log=79#/Pages/79`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0191.json__f46c72674e9980a9?doc=primary&log=80#/Pages/80`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0191.json__f46c72674e9980a9?doc=primary&log=81#/Pages/81`.

Haru asks Player61; he agrees but proposes carrying bags back to the office62. Kajika and Haru explicitly include him63/64, and Musubi says gift exchange is for everyone65. Player changes his plan to joint shopping66; the three affirm67. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0192.json__50f60e807224e038?doc=primary&log=58#/Pages/58`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0192.json__50f60e807224e038?doc=primary&log=67#/Pages/67`.
- **EV-B0248-083 / P68-69:** Player's closing narration68 says all Christmas-party preparations were finished and party day was near. Preserve that narrated closure rather than leaving the entire preparation phase pending. The actual party, exchanged gifts, exact purchases, costume appearance, solo act, illusion outcome and resolution of earlier participant objections are not supplied by the summary or closing white fade69. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0192.json__50f60e807224e038?doc=primary&log=68#/Pages/68`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0192.json__50f60e807224e038?doc=primary&log=69#/Pages/69`.

#### Interpretation and alternatives

**CL-B0248-119 — The ending gives preparation its own narrative shape**

The ending gives preparation its own narrative shape. Errands and costume fabric supply practical progress, while gift choice reveals the labor of anticipating an unknown recipient's preferences. The story closes this phase explicitly yet withholds the party, so narrative completion of preparation must not be inflated into successful performance or universal assent.

**CL-B0248-120 — Musubi's gift deliberation makes seriousness a concrete relational practice: a limited budget, a wish for lasting usefulness and concern about another person's taste**

Musubi's gift deliberation makes seriousness a concrete relational practice: a limited budget, a wish for lasting usefulness and concern about another person's taste. Peer recognition reframes its burden as care without ridiculing her as incapable. Her decision to ease up remains tentative/local, and the original solo-act dispute is not answered.

Player's interpretation of Haru is initially a hypothesis41 and becomes locally supported only through Haru's confirmation42. This offers a positive example of interpretive repair unlike the family's recurrent untested assumptions about another person's preferences. It does not make Player an omniscient interpreter of all members.

**CL-B0248-122 — The family repeatedly distributes knowledge unevenly**

The family repeatedly distributes knowledge unevenly. Here the manager is included in gift exchange only after learning of a lesson announcement he missed. Haru's forgotten task is distinguished from Player's lack of notification, and Musubi's apology for venting is distinguished from acceptance of Coney's unconsulted decision.

Haru/Kajika's coordinated laughter and sister simile show perceived affinity inside an expressly temporary collaboration, not a new kinship identity. Musubi's envy of having a partner reveals the experiential difference between collective and solo assignments, even as she remains an active member after her earlier quitting announcement.

The final invitation converts Player from errands carrier proposing withdrawal into an explicitly invited fellow participant. The subsequent narration closes preparation, while details of the purchases and later reception remain open.

#### Questions and limits at this original exit

- Which gifts were purchased and exchanged, and what was their reception?
- What costume and act will the Christmas-limited Haru/Kajika duo actually present?

### Complete-family causal and epistemic reconstruction

#### Factual reconstruction

- The complete admitted family is six documents labelled 前編 Chapter1–6,430native pages. It follows a proposed Nanasta Christmas party through assignments, visits to preparations, material costs, costume and illusion disputes, errands and gift planning. The final narration explicitly says preparations are complete and party day near; this family does not depict the party itself.
- Chapter1: Coney proposes appreciation and a rest from normal work, but makes members the performers and assigns acts by darts. Suggestions and reactions vary. Musubi objects to the solo-manzai allocation and formally announces quitting/graduation, while her portrait and peer exchange continue; no completed resignation or departure is supplied.
- Chapter2: Haru/Kajika describe an expressly Christmas-limited duo and seek energy from other preparations. Sawara's practiced tablecloth pull is textually represented as a success with dishes/glasses remaining. Shizuka's later attempt is followed by a supplied crash cue and Haru's report that the items broke. Prior practice losses, cleanup requests and Haru's return with tools make material labor visible. Crystal identification and a speculative price frighten Player without producing a bill, settled value or liability contract.
- Chapter3: Shinju trusts Shizuka's archery and suction-cup plan but objects to the fairy costume. Shizuka reports grandfather's costume provision, and Kajika/Haru express wanting to see it; Shinju twice rejects others deciding for her. No shot, costume delivery/change, affirmative costume assent or acted show is given.
- Chapter4: Hime/Shizuka rehearse an MC script naming Team Illusion's four-person lineup. Shizuka contributes three written puns despite her role anxiety; Hime locally accepts an ad lib and voices worry. Hime chooses to keep her street-performance content secret but openly prefers ordinary clothes. A costume pledge is delayed-attributed to grandfather. Haru's fondness appraisal and Player's sponsor simile do not create a formal sponsor or Hime's acceptance.
- Chapter5: Momoka/Susu initially invoke spectator roles; proposed work is differentiated into Rona inside an illusion box, Sui assisting, Momoka directing and Susu helping. Rona's repeated fear/appeals persist as remembered media spectacle escalates to knives, saw, chains and fire. Momoka admits not knowing the trick; Player's gentler-plan and fabrication objections fail to change the proposal. An email-mediated report says grandfather lent a box. Sui/Susu cheer, Rona still seeks help. Momoka finally denies hypnosis expertise and reframes it as a sleep-technique joke; no hypnotic state or illusion is enacted.
- Chapter6: supplies and costume-fabric additions are reported bought; Musubi remains present and objects to the solo act. Gift exchange is new information to Player and forgotten information for Haru. Musubi's detailed concern for an unknown recipient receives positive peer interpretation, locally confirmed by Haru, and she tentatively relaxes. All four present agree to joint gift shopping. Player's closing narration compresses the end of preparation, leaving actual gifts, acts and party outcomes outside this family.

#### Causal structure

- Coney's appreciation plan creates a common occasion, but undisclosed performer duties and dart assignments turn leisure into preparation labor. The story's varied responses are causal evidence: they generate visits, role disputes, uncertainty about skill, costume making, errands and deliberation. Collective enthusiasm must not replace those separate motivations.
- Haru/Kajika's temporary collaboration, with a theme they explicitly chose inside Coney's planning assignment, links the visits. They solicit others' energy and later report turning it into costume work. Their positive undertaking contrasts with Musubi's isolated assignment and the costume/illusion preferences of Shinju, Hime and Rona. One source-grounded ensemble can carry both voluntary creativity and pressure.
- Grandfather's resources recur through several channels: borrowed tableware with breakage; reported costume provision for Shinju and Hime; and a reported loaned illusion box. These acts can enable preparation and remove cost/fabrication obstacles without resolving a participant's wishes, establishing safety, fixing liability or revealing a formal sponsorship. Do not turn a repeated resource into an all-knowing hidden producer.
- The strongest enacted technical contrast is local: Sawara's represented tablecloth success follows prior practice, while Shizuka's attempt breaks items after vague instruction. The proposed archery, hidden street act and illusion lack equivalent execution evidence. Distinguish narrated local result, actor-reported expertise, planned effect and untested prediction.
- Gift discussion supplies a bounded positive repair. Player's interpretation of Haru's appraisal is a proposal until Haru confirms it; Musubi receives recognition of her recipient-oriented seriousness and opts for less strain. Explicit inclusion of Player yields joint assent to shopping. This does not retroactively solve the earlier solo/costume/illusion objections.
- Formal closure belongs to preparation, not to every proposed mechanism or conflict. The closing narration may certify that preparations ended at its stated level while leaving which acts, costumes and personal preferences were finally realized unshown. The next family remains semantically unopened and cannot supply retrospective certainty here.

#### Epistemic boundaries

- Coney's framing, Player's managerial knowledge and members' notification are not identical. The performer condition arrives late inChapter1; Player later reports no consultation about solo manzai and no knowledge of the lesson-announced gift exchange. Haru's forgotten known task and Player's missing notification have different acquisition histories.
- Shizuka's local archery/costume assertions and reports of grandfather are not his direct own turns. InChapter4 his pledge is attributed only after its first-person phrasing; inChapter5 Momoka quotes an email attributed to Shizuka. Preserve quoted speaker, reporting speaker, knower and inspected event separately.
- Rona's building-fear misunderstanding is explicitly corrected as a joke, but her later fear follows new proposals and remains unresolved. Sui's trust, Susu's remembered videos and Momoka's qualified assurances are not technical verification or Rona's assent.
- Apparent hypnosis knowledge is constructed by ignored qualification and a reinforced claim, then revised by Momoka's explicit denial and written 催眠術/睡眠術 reframe. Haru directly receives this correction. Native slot changes do not prove other members left or could not hear; no actual induced sleep or hypnotic performance is shown.
- Hime's specific street act remains withheld at her choice. Shinju's confidence in the archery plan is not consent to a costume. Musubi's apology for venting is not approval of solo manzai. The ending preparation summary supplies no missing affirmative decisions for these distinct propositions.
- Musubi's quitting/graduation announcement is preserved as speech, not accomplished departure. Chapter6's actual continued involvement does not require inventing an exit, return or formal re-admission. This remains consistent with the older REV-0044 distinction.
- Predicted shopping by absent Momoka/Susu, grandfather's Nanasta affinity and the sponsor/sister comparisons remain character appraisals, predictions or similes. No exact calendar date, formal institution/kinship, price, invoice or private universal motive is supplied.

#### Character relations

- Haru/Kajika: an explicitly Christmas-limited planned duo, with shared initiative and costume work, later perceived as sister-like. New bounded UNIT_STATE is justified by explicit formation/roles, while biological identity, permanent branding, performed act and unit-synthesis maturity remain open.
- Momoka/Susu/Sui/Rona: Team Illusion's planned membership is first announced in a rehearsal script, then own turns expose uneven roles, partial enthusiasm, mistaken expertise and Rona's continuing refusal. A bounded collective row must record that disagreement rather than certify unanimous membership consent, a safe trick or an accomplished performance.
- Shizuka/Sawara/Shinju/Hime/grandfather: local trust, prior household authority, artistic determination and provision coexist with breakage and costume preferences. Insightful or generous care does not automatically transfer decision rights. Shizuka's own MC wordplay and Hime's secret act preserve creative contribution rather than reducing either to a pressure victim or sole coercer.
- Musubi/Haru/Kajika/Player: the ongoing solo grievance, envy of companionship, apology, gift concern, meaning repair and mutual thanks create a textured local relation. The positive advice is actually accepted for gift choice; broad cure, permanent dependence, solved act assignment or departure is not established.
- Coney/Player/ensemble: the leader's appreciation language and material planning coexist with unilateral assignments and notification gaps. Player offers care and practical intervention but is not shown successfully stopping the box proposal, resolving every consent issue or mastering every administrative fact.
- Susu/Player: the hug call, shake/SFX instructions and explicit separation request preserve a body boundary. No heard or runtime contact, release, injury, reciprocal romance or character essence is inferred.

#### Literary synthesis and rivals

**CL-B0248-125 — Appreciation becomes work while leaving room for chosen creativity**

Coney's appreciative party frame changes when members learn they must entertain and darts allocate acts. The resulting objections are not erased by a collective cheer. Haru and Kajika nevertheless take up their limited collaboration with a theme they say they chose, and turn others' energy into costume preparation. The coexistence of chosen craft and assigned burden is the family's texture, not a proof that every pleasure is false or every role accepted.
